# Docker

Every project across all 39 lists that ships a container image, a Dockerfile or a compose file — **2,023** of 11,022 distinct repos, deduplicated to one row per repo.

For building a container environment. Membership is evidence-based, not aspirational: a row is here because something in the repo or its README actually names a container.

[← back to the mega list](../README.md)

Page **2** of 3, because this list is longer than the 512 KB GitHub will render in one file. In order: [1](docker.md) · **2** · [3](docker-3.md) — [continue on page 3 →](docker-3.md)
